# DOM API INVENTORY — Technical Audit & Remediation Catalog

## Executive Summary

This inventory documents every instance of imperative DOM manipulation across the AlgoRecall extension codebase on the `preact` branch. A compiler-grade AST analysis across 73 TypeScript and TSX files identified **1,495 imperative DOM operations** spanning **51 files**, alongside **6 manually maintained DOM element cache structures**.

During the prior migration attempt, Preact components were introduced into HTML shells (`<div id="root"></div>`), but the codebase fell into a **"Facade Migration"** anti-pattern:
1. Companion Vanilla JS controller classes and legacy procedural methods were retained in full.
2. Preact components pass class instances via props (e.g., `manager={this}`), delegating event handling and mutations back to legacy classes.
3. Legacy classes imperatively query the DOM tree rendered by Preact using `document.getElementById` and `querySelector`.
4. State is bifurcated ("Split-Brain State"): UI state is stored on class instances and pushed into the DOM via `innerHTML`, `style.display`, and `classList` mutations rather than flowing down declaratively via Preact props and hooks.
5. Injected content scripts (`tracker.tsx`, `highlighter.tsx`, `notifications.ts`) inject raw HTML strings and attach manual event listeners instead of mounting Preact component trees into injected containers.

---

## High-Level Metrics & Category Breakdown

### Overall Metrics
- **Total TS/TSX Source Files Audited**: 73 files
- **Files Containing Imperative DOM Operations**: 51 files (69.8% of codebase)
- **Total Imperative DOM Operations**: 1,495
- **Manually Maintained DOM Element Caches**: 6 structures (65 cached element fields)

### Classification Breakdown

| Classification Category | File Count | DOM Operations | Compliance Status | Remediation Required |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **Extension-Owned UI (Page Controllers & Modals)** | 41 | 1,213 | ❌ Violation | Eliminate completely; refactor to declarative Preact components & hooks |
| **Extension-Owned UI (Injected Widget Body - Tracker)** | 1 | 150 | ❌ Violation | Refactor internal widget UI to declarative Preact functional components |
| **Extension-Owned UI (Injected Tooltip - Highlighter)** | 1 | 41 | ❌ Violation | Refactor tooltip controls to declarative Preact functional components |
| **Extension-Owned UI (Form Controls - TagInput)** | 1 | 20 | ❌ Violation | Eliminate TagInputControl class; replace with declarative `<TagInput />` |
| **Extension-Owned UI (Legacy Helpers - UIUtils)** | 1 | 2 | ❌ Violation | Eliminate imperative DOM error banner in favor of Preact Toast context |
| **Content Script Injection (Host DOM Boundary)** | 5 | 62 | ⚠️ Technically Justified | Retain container creation in host DOM; mount Preact roots into containers |
| **Legitimate Utility (Download Anchor Trick)** | 2 | 4 | ✅ Technically Justified | Retain hidden `<a>` download fallback behind BROWSER_CONFIG guard |
| **Legitimate Utility (Pre-Mount FOUC Prevention)** | 1 | 3 | ✅ Technically Justified | Retain synchronous `<head>` classList sync on documentElement |
| **TOTALS** | **51** | **1,495** | | **1,426 operations to eliminate / refactor** |

---

### Operations by API Pattern

| API Pattern | Total Occurrences | Typical Misuse | Declarative Preact Replacement |
| :--- | :---: | :--- | :--- |
| `document.getElementById` | 610 | Querying Preact-rendered inputs, buttons, and containers | Controlled component props, Preact hooks (`useState`), JSX event handlers, `useRef` |
| `addEventListener` | 257 | Manual event binding on buttons, inputs, tabs, selects | JSX event props (`onClick`, `onInput`, `onChange`, `onKeyDown`) |
| `element.style.*` mutations | 141 | Imperative `display = 'none'/'block'`, positions, colors | Conditional rendering (`{isOpen && <Modal />}`), inline JSX `style={{...}}` |
| `element.classList.*` mutations | 130 | Toggling `active`, `show`, `visible`, `light-theme` | Dynamic JSX className (`className={\`tab ${isActive ? 'active' : ''}\`}`) |
| `textContent` / `innerText` | 121 | Setting titles, status messages, counter labels | Declarative JSX expressions (`<h1>{title}</h1>`, `<span>{count}</span>`) |
| `innerHTML` / `outerHTML` | 80 | Injecting HTML strings, empty state cards, tables | Declarative Preact components and JSX fragments |
| `querySelector` / `querySelectorAll` | 81 | Scoping searches for buttons, checkboxes, tabs | Component composition, `Array.map()`, child component props |
| `createElement` / `appendChild` / `removeChild` | 63 | Programmatic node creation and destruction | Declarative JSX rendering; conditional VNodes |
| `removeEventListener` | 6 | Manual teardown of window/document listeners | `useEffect` cleanup returns (`return () => window.removeEventListener(...)`) |

## Section 1: Manually Maintained DOM Element Caches

The audit identified manually constructed DOM element cache interfaces and objects where controllers query the DOM once during initialization and retain mutable element pointers:

### 1. `PopupDOM` Interface & Cache (`features/dashboard/popup/popup.tsx`)
- **Location**: `features/dashboard/popup/popup.tsx:792-845` (Interface), `874-900` (First instantiation), `927-960` (Second instantiation).
- **Cached Elements (28 fields)**:
  - Toggles: `markerToggle`, `chartsToggle`, `dailyFocusToggle`, `launcherToggle`, `devModeToggle`
  - Navigation Buttons: `helpBtn`, `historyBtn`, `openHeatmapTabBtn`, `manageHighlightsBtn`, `openOptionsBtn`, `analyticsBtn`, `headerAnalyticsBtn`, `forecastBtn`, `achievementsBtn`, `studyplanBtn`, `pomodoroBtn`, `openSummaryPageBtn`, `settingsHubBtn`, `themeToggleBtn`
  - Stat Containers: `boxTotal`, `boxDue`, `boxRetention`
  - Status & Developer Containers: `statusMsg`, `devModeActions`, `exportDebugLogsBtn`, `testNotificationsContainer`
- **Impact**: All of these elements are simultaneously declared in Preact components (`<PopupApp />`, `<DashboardComponent />`) and queried imperatively in `PopupDashboard.bindEvents()`.
- **Refactoring Strategy**: Delete the `PopupDOM` interface and `this.dom` object entirely. Convert `PopupApp` into the top-level state holder using `useStorage` and Preact hooks, passing state and callbacks directly to JSX components.

### 2. `SettingsController.dom` Cache (`features/common/settings/settings.tsx`)
- **Location**: `features/common/settings/settings.tsx:1445-1485` (Type definition), `1488-1527` (Initialization).
- **Cached Elements (32 fields)**:
  - Nav Tabs & Panels: `tabs` (querySelectorAll), `panels` (querySelectorAll)
  - Toggles: `toggleNotifications`, `toggleSticky`, `toggleQuietHours`, `toggleWeeklyDigest`, `toggleBadgeDueCount`, `toggleMarker`, `toggleLauncher`, `toggleShowCharts`, `toggleDailyFocus`, `toggleDevMode`
  - Selects & Inputs: `notificationInterval`, `customIntervalInput`, `quietHoursStart`, `quietHoursEnd`, `importFile`, `ankiImportFile`
  - Action Buttons & Containers: `exportBtn`, `ankiExportBtn`, `testNotificationBtn`, `testSummaryBtn`, `exportDebugLogsBtn`, `rateStoreBtn`, `toast`, `themeToggleBtn`, `customIntervalContainer`, `quietHoursContainer`, `devIntervalOption`, `devActionsContainer`
- **Impact**: `SettingsApp` renders all 7 panel components via Preact, but `SettingsController` retains this 32-element cache to imperatively attach `change`/`click` listeners and toggle panel visibility.
- **Refactoring Strategy**: Delete `this.dom` and `SettingsController`. Provide a `useSettings()` hook managing storage synchronization; render active panels declaratively via `{activeTab === 'notifications' && <NotificationsPanel ... />}`.

### 3. `TagInputControl` Element Cache (`features/common/utils/tagInput.tsx`)
- **Location**: `features/common/utils/tagInput.tsx:135-148`.
- **Cached Elements (5 fields)**:
  - `private targetInput: HTMLInputElement`
  - `private wrapperEl!: HTMLDivElement`
  - `private chipsContainerEl!: HTMLDivElement`
  - `private inlineInputEl!: HTMLInputElement`
  - `private dropdownEl!: HTMLDivElement`
- **Impact**: Wraps a hidden input element and imperatively intercepts property getters/setters while rendering sub-components into child nodes.
- **Refactoring Strategy**: Replace `TagInputControl` class with a declarative `<TagInput tags={tags} onChange={setTags} suggestions={...} />` Preact component.

## Section 3: Pattern-Specific Inventories & Key Risk Areas

### 1. HTML String Injections (`innerHTML` / `outerHTML` — 80 occurrences)
- **Files Affected**: `data.tsx`, `popup.tsx`, `analytics.tsx`, `futureMemorySimulation.tsx`, `memory.tsx`, `retentionChart.tsx`, `heatmap.tsx`, `pomodoro.tsx`, `tracker.tsx`, `highlighter.tsx`, `notifications.ts`, `highlights.tsx`.
- **Architectural Violation**: Bypasses Preact virtual DOM diffing, destroys and re-creates DOM nodes, triggers layout thrashing, and creates XSS surface if unescaped strings are used.
- **Remediation Strategy**: Replace every raw HTML template string with typed Preact JSX components.

### 2. Manual UI Event Listeners (`addEventListener` — 257 occurrences)
- **Files Affected**: `popup.tsx`, `data.tsx`, `settings.tsx`, `pomodoro.tsx`, `tracker.tsx`, `fsrsConfig.tsx`, `editor.tsx`, `welcome.tsx`, `help.tsx`, etc.
- **Architectural Violation**: Listeners are attached imperatively after DOM querying. They often fail to clean up during re-renders or unmounts, causing memory leaks and zombie callbacks.
- **Remediation Strategy**: Replace with declarative JSX event props (`onClick`, `onChange`, `onInput`, `onKeyDown`). For global listeners (window/document), wrap strictly in `useEffect(() => { window.addEventListener(...); return () => window.removeEventListener(...); }, [])`.

### 3. Direct Style & ClassList Mutations (271 occurrences)
- **Files Affected**: `popup.tsx`, `pomodoro.tsx`, `tracker.tsx`, `settings.tsx`, `highlighter.tsx`, `welcome.tsx`, `help.tsx`.
- **Architectural Violation**: Setting `style.display = 'none'/'block'` or `classList.toggle('active')` directly on DOM nodes creates a desynchronization between Preact's VNode tree and the real DOM.
- **Remediation Strategy**: Drive CSS classes and visibility through Preact props and state (`className={\`tab ${isActive ? 'active' : ''}\`}`, `{isVisible && <Component />}`).

## Section 5: Implementation Guidelines for Preact Developers

1. **Root Mounting Pattern**:
   Each extension page HTML entry must have exactly one mount point: `<div id="root"></div>`.
   Mount exactly once via:
   ```tsx
   const root = document.getElementById('root');
   if (root) {
       render(<AppRoot />, root);
   }
   ```
   Never invoke `render()` inside helper functions to replace sub-nodes.

2. **No Imperative DOM Queries**:
   Never call `document.getElementById`, `querySelector`, or `querySelectorAll` inside a component or hook.
   Use Preact's `useRef<HTMLElement>(null)` only when measuring DOM bounding rects or calling focus APIs.

3. **Controlled Form Inputs**:
   Never query `input.value` imperatively. Use Preact controlled components:
   ```tsx
   const [val, setVal] = useState('');
   <input value={val} onInput={(e) => setVal((e.target as HTMLInputElement).value)} />
   ```

4. **Event Handling**:
   Use JSX event props (`onClick`, `onChange`, `onSubmit`). Never call `element.addEventListener`.

5. **Conditional Rendering vs. `style.display`**:
   Never mutate `style.display = 'none'`. Use JSX conditional rendering:
   ```tsx
   {isOpen && <Modal onClose={() => setIsOpen(false)} />}
   ```
